The controller\n// will call its WaitForSync prior to starting workers.\ntype SyncingSource interface {\n\tSource\n\tWaitForSync(ctx context.Context) error\n}\n\n// Kind creates a KindSource with the given cache provider.\nfunc Kind(cache cache.Cache, object client.Object) SyncingSource {\n\treturn &internal.Kind{Type: object, Cache: cache}\n}\n\nvar _ Source = &Channel{}\n\n// Channel is used to provide a source of events originating outside the cluster\n// (e.g. GitHub Webhook callback).  Channel requires the user to wire the external\n// source (eh.g. http handler) to write GenericEvents to the underlying channel.\ntype Channel struct {\n\t// once ensures the event distribution goroutine will be performed only once\n\tonce sync.Once\n\n\t// Source is the source channel to fetch GenericEvents\n\tSource <-chan event.GenericEvent\n\n\t// dest is the destination channels of the added event handlers\n\tdest []chan event.GenericEvent\n\n\t// DestBufferSize is the specified buffer size of dest channels.\n\t// Default to 1024 if not specified.\n\tDestBufferSize int\n\n\t// destLock is to ensure the destination channels are safely added/removed\n\tdestLock sync.Mutex\n}\n\nfunc (cs *Channel) String() string {\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"channel source: %p\", cs)\n}\n\n// Start implements Source and should only be called by the Controller.\nfunc (cs *Channel) Start(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\thandler handler.EventHandler,\n\tqueue workqueue.RateLimitingInterface,\n\tprct ...predicate.Predicate) error {\n\t// Source should have been specified by the user.\n\tif cs.Source == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"must specify Channel.Source\")\n\t}\n\n\t// use default value if DestBufferSize not specified\n\tif cs.DestBufferSize == 0 {\n\t\tcs.DestBufferSize = defaultBufferSize\n\t}\n\n\tdst := make(chan event.GenericEvent, cs.DestBufferSize)\n\n\tcs.destLock.Lock()\n\tcs.dest = append(cs.dest, dst)\n\tcs.destLock.Unlock()\n\n\tcs.once.Do(func() {\n\t\t// Distribute GenericEvents to all EventHandler / Queue pairs Watching this source\n\t\tgo cs.syncLoop(ctx)\n\t})\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfor evt := range dst {\n\t\t\tshouldHandle := true\n\t\t\tfor _, p := range prct {\n\t\t\t\tif !p.Generic(evt) {\n\t\t\t\t\tshouldHandle = false\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif shouldHandle {\n\t\t\t\tfunc() {\n\t\t\t\t\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)\n\t\t\t\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t\t\t\t\thandler.Generic(ctx, evt, queue)\n\t\t\t\t}()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (cs *Channel) doStop() {\n\tcs.destLock.Lock()\n\tdefer cs.destLock.Unlock()\n\n\tfor _, dst := range cs.dest {\n\t\tclose(dst)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (cs *Channel) distribute(evt event.GenericEvent) {\n\tcs.destLock.Lock()\n\tdefer cs.destLock.Unlock()\n\n\tfor _, dst := range cs.dest {\n\t\t// We cannot make it under goroutine here, or we'll meet the\n\t\t// race condition of writing message to closed channels.\n\t\t// To avoid blocking, the dest channels are expected to be of\n\t\t// proper buffer size. If we still see it blocked, then\n\t\t// the controller is thought to be in an abnormal state.\n\t\tdst <- evt\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (cs *Channel) syncLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t// Close destination channels\n\t\t\tcs.doStop()\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase evt, stillOpen := <-cs.Source:\n\t\t\tif !stillOpen {\n\t\t\t\t// if the source channel is closed, we're never gonna get\n\t\t\t\t// anything more on it, so stop & bail\n\t\t\t\tcs.doStop()\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcs.distribute(evt)\n\t\t}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":180,"totalLines":225,"truncated":true}},"title":"../go/pkg/mod/sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime@v0.15.0/pkg/source/source.go","time":{"start":1782063866966,"end":1782063866982}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0762fee30123234a016a3822fa72a481908651cb529747a734"}},"id":"prt_eeb48a2ec001uTDgcRB6DFpT64","sessionID":"ses_114bf0736ffefOzHO1dhYFWrc3","messageID":"msg_eeb4874a90012MxY4NVpXx47T3"}}
